Append one entry at the end of every working turn. Record actions, results, repairs and the exact continuation point. Times use Europe/London.

Recorded all 39,278 tracked paths and object identities in `reference/V3_TRACKED_FILES.tsv`; pinned commit and census hash in `reference/SOURCE.json`. Did not substitute the earlier report's 2,777 count.

10. Replaced simplification proofs with explicit constructor, equality and list-membership proofs in Root and One. Rebuilt: PASS, 8 jobs. All 13 audited declarations reported no axiom dependencies. Preserved `verification/TURN_001_FINAL_BUILD.log`.
11. Received the additional instruction to maintain the accompanying draft Zenodo paper every working turn. Updated local agent instructions and created `ZENODO_PAPER_DRAFT.md` with authorship, source baseline, methods, actual proved results, verification counts, reproducibility and publication status.
12. Updated coverage: 3 claims in progress, 2,778 pending, 0 claims claimed fully reconstructed with artifact/runtime parity. Checked unique census IDs, absence of custom axioms/unfinished proofs in current Lean sources, and all thirteen final no-axiom audit outputs.

14. Recorded source hashes and exact continuation in `verification/TURN_001_CHECKPOINT.json`. Wrote this timeline entry and aligned the manuscript with that checkpoint. No V3 source edits, Git commits, remote writes or publication actions occurred.

**Next exact step:** Complete `SFT-FOUNDATION-COUNT-001`: its ten-class candidate grammar, exhaustive enumeration, unique survivor, adverse cases and base/successor generation-trace preservation.
